```yaml
lines:
  - id: f2441.h_providers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons or organizations who provided the care (Part I, line 1)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dcprov, field: dcprov.one}

  - id: f2441.line1_box
    printed: "1 (box)"
    label: "If you have more than three care providers, see the instructions and check this box"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f2441.h_providers, {param: dc.providers_listed}]}

  - id: f2441.h_qp_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons (line 2)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f2441.nondependent, f2441.student_disabled]
    note: "Qualifying persons are counted from the dependent entries. A disabled spouse is screened by f2441.student_disabled. A qualifying person who is not your dependent is screened by f2441.nondependent. The tool refuses both cases instead of counting those persons."

  - id: f2441.line2_box
    printed: "2 (box)"
    label: "Information about your qualifying person(s). If you have more than three qualifying persons, see the instructions and check this box"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f2441.h_qp_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}

  - id: f2441.h_col_d
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of the amounts in column (d) of line 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.dc_line2d}
    refuses: [f2441.nondependent, f2441.student_disabled]

  - id: f2441.h_two
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Two or more qualifying persons"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [f2441.h_qp_count, {param: dc.persons_two}]}

  - id: f2441.h_limit_one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$3,000 (one qualifying person)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.limit_one}

  - id: f2441.h_limit_two
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$6,000 (two or more qualifying persons)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.limit_two}

  - id: f2441.h_dollar_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$3,000 if one qualifying person, $6,000 if two or more"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.h_two, {const: true}]}
          then: f2441.h_limit_two
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.h_two, {const: false}]}
          then: f2441.h_limit_one

  - id: f2441.h_line3_part2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (d) total, not more than $3,000 ($6,000 if two or more persons)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.h_col_d, f2441.h_dollar_limit]}

  - id: f2441.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Add the amounts in column (d) of line 2. Don't enter more than $3,000 if you had one qualifying person or $6,000 if you had two or more persons. If you completed Part III, enter the amount from line 31"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.benefits, {const: true}]}
          then: f2441.line31
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.benefits, {const: false}]}
          then: f2441.h_line3_part2
    note: "Part III is completed exactly when dependent care benefits were received (f2441.benefits)."

  - id: f2441.line9b
    printed: "9b"
    label: "If you paid 2024 expenses in 2025, complete Worksheet A in the instructions. Enter the amount from line 13 of the worksheet here. Otherwise, enter -0- on line 9b and go to line 9c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [f2441.prior_year]
    note: "Worksheet A (2024 expenses paid in 2025) is screened by f2441.prior_year and refused. Only the -0- case is computed."

  - id: f2441.h_12_13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 and 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f2441.line12, f2441.line13]}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits

  - id: f2441.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Combine lines 12 through 14. See instructions"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f2441.h_12_13, take: f2441.line14}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits

  - id: f2441.h_dc_paid_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified expenses incurred and paid in 2025 for the qualifying persons"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.dc_paid
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f2441.nondependent, f2441.student_disabled]

  - id: f2441.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Enter the total amount of qualified expenses incurred in 2025 for the care of the qualifying person(s)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f2441.h_dc_paid_sum, f2441.paid_2026]}
    refuses: [f2441.employer_care]
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "Expenses incurred in 2025 are counted whenever they were paid. That is the per-person amounts incurred and paid in 2025 plus the other 2025-incurred expenses (f2441.paid_2026). Care your employer furnished itself is not counted by this tool, so that case is refused."

  - id: f2441.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Enter the smaller of line 15 or 16"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.line15, f2441.line16]}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits

  - id: f2441.h_excl_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.exclusion_max}

  - id: f2441.h_excl_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,500 (married filing separately)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f2441.h_excl_max, rate: {param: sa.half}}

  - id: f2441.h_mfs
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}

  - id: f2441.h_excl_status
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000 ($2,500 if married filing separately and you were required to enter your spouse's earned income on line 19)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.h_mfs, {const: true}]}
          then: f2441.h_excl_half
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.h_mfs, {const: false}]}
          then: f2441.h_excl_max
    note: "Open point: the $2,500 amount applies to a married-filing-separately filer only if they had to enter the spouse's earned income on line 19, that is, if they are not considered unmarried. The closed interface has no input for that determination. Chosen reading: every married-filing-separately return uses $2,500. This understates line 21 for a separate filer who is considered unmarried."

  - id: f2441.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Enter $5,000 ($2,500 if married filing separately and you were required to enter your spouse's earned income on line 19). However, don't enter more than the maximum amount allowed under your dependent care plan. See instructions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.h_excl_status, f2441.plan_max]}
    refuses: [f2441.plans_differ]
    shown_when: f2441.benefits

  - id: f2441.h_20_21
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 20 or line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.line20, f2441.line21]}

  - id: f2441.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Excluded benefits. If you checked \"No\" on line 22, enter the smaller of line 20 or line 21. Otherwise, subtract line 24 from the smaller of line 20 or line 21. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f2441.h_20_21, take: f2441.line24}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "When 'No' is checked on line 22, line 22 is -0-, so line 24 (the smallest of lines 20, 21 and 22) is zero. Subtracting line 24 then gives the smaller of lines 20 and 21, so one expression covers both branches."

  - id: f2441.line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Enter $3,000 ($6,000 if two or more qualifying persons)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f2441.h_dollar_limit}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits

  - id: f2441.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Complete line 2 on page 1 of this form. Don't include in column (d) any benefits shown on line 28 above. Then, add the amounts in column (d) and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f2441.h_col_d, take: f2441.line28}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "Open point: column (d) must leave out the benefits shown on line 28. The per-person expense input includes expenses paid with dependent care benefits ('Form 2441 subtracts the benefits you exclude'). Chosen reading: line 28 is subtracted from the column (d) total, with a floor of zero."
